Healoo server (care-api): third-party Rust libraries
=====================================================

Generated by Server/tools/third_party_licenses.py from the packages Cargo resolves for
Server/care-api. Do not edit by hand; run the script again after changing dependencies.

The list covers every platform Cargo knows about, so it includes libraries that are not
compiled into the Linux server (for example Windows-only ones).
Where a library offers a choice of licences ("MIT OR Apache-2.0"), Healoo uses it under the
MIT licence, or else the first permissive one listed.

Libraries: 475
